                                💡 AI Executive Summary: Key News Insights
                            </div>
                            <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" width="100%" style="background-color: #F8FAFC; border-left: 4px solid #FF9900; border: 1px solid #E2E8F0; border-left-width: 4px;">
                                <tr>
                                    <td style="padding: 20px 24px; font-size: 13.5px; color: #334155; line-height: 1.7em; text-align: justify;">
                                        <div style='margin-top:2px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>1. 러시아 스베르방크 메가 데이터센터 피격과 국가 핵심 인프라 물리 안보 위기</div>우크라이나가 얀덱스에 이어 러시아 최대 국영은행 스베르방크의 사라토프주 발라코보 메가 데이터센터를 드론으로 정밀 타격함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMisAFBVV95cUxQSUNHemlDaEdIV3JVYVVUZ1VxdFpXN2FmRVdYNWZBTVVRUlNLWnJ3ai0xXzFrU0ptc3lIRXJvUklfanY5bElFM09aMmNabVJQRExhZzZ0MW0tUFVBb0wwOVhCdWVoRWZJZGpEcUlIZUxRWXllZ3Vxdk1VdFc1ZWJRT25rWngwMjlEVEVqR2dGbDdDaktGTDhWOWI1cE9jU3dvMnNCQnZQMi1saGVuQXk5eQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[1]</a>. 차세대 국가 AI 슈퍼컴퓨터 배치가 예정되었던 전략 거점이 파괴되자 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiyAFBVV95cUxPN1RPWlRvTjJaTVgwSXYxNGpNLW4zM0J0RVJWeWhuYXFOS29aSGpFRGVvVjVhUEJFa2Q2VkFrR1JkY05Ga0FUcWpJNGtkeFNnV2NsSkltakhfVk55WWg1anVtZGhQUHNCYnB5U0lza1kzdUxDeEt3OGNuS2VzMk8yRVB1OWR2VHFVNWVXblYwdkN6Z1FjcXNTczk0UHJkanoyekVCTDlDTDRfWi04LTV3a0ZqMUp5NTVSMnM3ZlRCNDc3cnhtZ3FUcdIByAFBVV95cUxPN1RPWlRvTjJaTVgwSXYxNGpNLW4zM0J0RVJWeWhuYXFOS29aSGpFRGVvVjVhUEJFa2Q2VkFrR1JkY05Ga0FUcWpJNGtkeFNnV2NsSkltakhfVk55WWg1anVtZGhQUHNCYnB5U0lza1kzdUxDeEt3OGNuS2VzMk8yRVB1OWR2VHFVNWVXblYwdkN6Z1FjcXNTczk0UHJkanoyekVCTDlDTDRfWi04LTV3a0ZqMUp5NTVSMnM3ZlRCNDc3cnhtZ3FUcQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[2]</a> 러시아 전역에서 광범위한 인터넷 통신망 불통과 금융 결제 마비가 발생했고 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=A large-scale internet outage occurred in Russia following a drone attack on Sberbank's data center&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[3]</a> 10억 달러(약 1조 3,500억 원) 규모의 핵심 자산이 심각한 타격을 입음 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiwAFBVV95cUxONEtLMmdUMlBmQ1VfRG1VWUM1TFRzcllaREtITHZjU0xzSndSRjU2czZNNnVXM0ozTWtVTDVGS21wZnI3d2MtRzJYZ3l1SmdiSGNtTWV0T3NPYlVGNDVOZmhLU0lVR3J2QmtfZXlpdzhkMDlEN3pBZzZvSWhiSmUwNllleHVIS0NuTjR3VC11SzdxY2Jpb1ZFNXh3N3M4a0FwZVRmME1VSnl0bVZNemM2b0diOGNrNEtaODI1VkhnS2M?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[4]</a>. 나흘 새 연쇄 공습으로 디지털 중추가 마비되자 러시아 내부에서는 핵 공격에도 견디는 지하 방호형 둠스데이 데이터센터 구축론까지 급부상함 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=In Russia, following Ukrainian attacks on data centers, there were calls to create a "doomsday" data center&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[5]</a>. 이러한 하이브리드 전시 표적화는 글로벌 전역으로 확산되어 이란이 걸프 해저케이블과 데이터센터 타격을 공식 위협하고 <a href='https://www.chosun.com/international/international_general/2026/10/14/GUZGCYRRGIYGMNZRG42DMZJZMU/' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[6]</a> 폴란드 내 스타링크 지상국 방화 사건 배후로 러시아 연계 세력이 지목되는 등 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Two Turkish nationals arrested over Russian-linked arson attack against Starlink ground station in Poland&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[7]</a> 디지털 인프라가 비대칭 군사 공격의 최우선 타깃으로 변모함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMikgFBVV95cUxPRU4tc0NOTkdtdTFyYzNHWGV1V09Yck9KTm03OG1xWXpBZVE1UzQ3Vm9FejQ3eDVtWnlSeTlfNjc2c3k2d3R0X3JWNjg1ckh4RUppazBOV1JCU05ha3pxWW1PbFl3UVVadW9HRDJQQUNPWWFhSlN1TXV6WG45WTlXT1RDd3NfUTZLOHNYYURLNWFFUQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[8]</a>. 국내에서도 얀덱스와 스베르방크 사태를 계기로 <a href='https://www.newsis.com/view/NISX20261014_0003825659' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[9]</a> 물리적 망 단절과 외부 공격 리스크에 대응해 소버린 클라우드 다중화와 방호 체계 재점검에 돌입함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMic0FVX3lxTE9reUVKYzZoVHVqN2dMSXVwTXFtTnVtTGN3SjFaYjJ1UFZQUlg4N1lYSTFkem9mampZbDJYTWNacTRPbjV2NWhHOVJCVDFkdE52UWtTMzN5VnBLZmE3dWNBeDMyX0x2OTN3bE1tYnVrdFNEUlXSAXNBVV95cUxPa3lFSmM2aFR1ajdnTEl1cE1xbU51bUxjd0oxWmIydVBWUFJYODdZWEkxZHpvZmpqWWwyWE1jWnE0T241djVoRzlSQlQxZHROdlFrUzMzeVZwS2ZhN3VjQXgzMl9Mdjkzd2xNbWJ1a3RTRFJV?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[10]</a>.<div style='margin-top:18px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>2. LG전자·GS건설·GRC 액침냉각 표준화 동맹과 차세대 열관리 하드웨어 수주전</div>초고발열 AI 가속기 발열을 제어하기 위해 글로벌 냉각 시장이 액침(Immersion) 표준화로 급격히 전환되는 양상임 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiaEFVX3lxTE1OSThNeWlBZ2tiYncyQWR1Z2R5clB2NjFtSjJQVW9OZU9sMkdIcFNQbHhVSTZoWG1qcXNiVUYwX01kanZtWUtPTi1UN2Q4bVBwdk5ZRWRDMDZjejR6eEx6QkVLR1NpUUE3?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[11]</a>. 인프라 기업 3C가 데이터렉과 합병해 4억 달러(약 5,400억 원) 규모의 액체냉각 플랫폼을 구축했고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMitAFBVV95cUxQN250TEJzWUVHNGl2SEc3aVBvbmxWYWVFSlpCbkNFRmNzSkpWSkhybmdhc0lhakRJa21WYnltSW4yR2hwek9NenFNbkpYaldfQXVoaTB1VVlZUEYxVURrVlRoLXZtVWpqaERpc2ZoQzlKNHJueHNqM2xjdk1IcXZkOVdpbGkzNV9FNTc5cDFQODduM2tURFpvbFgxdWw1TFRlM1liNmRnQVlIMWduZXBqM3d1Zkg?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[12]</a> 쿨러마스터가 2,500kW급 냉각분배장치(CDU)를 공개했으며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMisAFBVV95cUxPS2NuTGNLSjdLV19ZQi1Qc01yZ0VGU2RPNVBKc0RocVBmWTlYeEdnX1ItZkxjbEk2QWhqRDBpR3ZzZmRaZ1hIQXR1YTgzZHh4MHhHOVF3dktBYzZZRm1qQk5SN2tJZWRya196SnlGbjVQaW16TDRnbjVGRWo2Q25DUmdtYWllN0wyeDZzd1FlcnR4dUYzei1TeTJ5R1lZRDY0NHFGcWMtek9NYXkxOFRoRQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[13]</a> 슈나이더 일렉트릭은 모티베어 기반 CDU를 출시함 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Schneider Electric launches new Motivair coolant distribution unit for more flexible data center deployments&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[14]</a>. 국내에서는 LG전자가 GS건설 및 미국 GRC와 손잡고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiWEFVX3lxTE82T0NwMWVVNGRybUF0bGZWUEVIaXdPX01WclUyUTBtakdfbllmS2JwYV9yQ3daOEhnbXdUal9fVWJSOHlfa3FNYThZNVZRdEVlM2JndEtKV1fSAVhBVV95cUxPNk9DcDFlVTRkcm1BdGxmVlBFSGl3T19NVnJVMlEwbWpHX25ZZkticGFfckN3WjhIZ213VGpfX1ViUjh5X2txTWE4WTVWUXRFZTNiZ3RLSldX?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[15]</a> 액침냉각 기술·표준설계 공동개발 협약을 체결함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMic0FVX3lxTE00c3RsczVQLUhrb2hldGo0c0xfR3BKcG9wT2luY0UwOE1vQmVvYko5V20wa0ptWUR6ak80N0dESmkxbVhTSWRzcXBSSlJUVnNQcnBoblJ4Y1NjWXowYVJfQkFwNzV4TExmUjdGc0M2ZVpkS2s?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[16]</a>. GPU 서버를 특수 냉각유에 직접 침전시키는 기술로 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMibEFVX3lxTE9TU25EbUFGTU8wQXZldWNWUjZPeGVLTnh2VlRWRWxFSkJoMEYxOUxfSlNNSTVBZndMblhTdU5zRWt5MjBtemVWeXRWMU93YkhraHhVMkZpX2ZoMzFDaTREZU0yNzI3UWd2Vkdjd9IBbEFVX3lxTE9TU25EbUFGTU8wQXZldWNWUjZPeGVLTnh2VlRWRWxFSkJoMEYxOUxfSlNNSTVBZndMblhTdU5zRWt5MjBtemVWeXRWMU93YkhraHhVMkZpX2ZoMzFDaTREZU0yNzI3UWd2Vkdjdw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[17]</a> LG전자의 냉각 설비와 GS건설의 건축·전력 설계 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMickFVX3lxTFB4UlVndTI1cFpESEVxTDB1YTdPdmRCS3VYWTE1dXc4TFp1S3V3eGROOXI1dnA0WDZ2X2ZMemhPR1V5M1ZsT2w5NVJHdHhaT2dxYVV3TUFRbWowTlZTczROb25FWHRidTZEY1FPaURtM1dwdw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[18]</a>, GRC의 액침 노하우를 결합해 표준 아키텍처를 검증하려는 포석임 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiZEFVX3lxTE5sb3FPdF9ocW90QXZRNmp5dU9wSTlWTWJLSEdsTFNDUDgwWjZSbC13NV9kMndnVkJmN1VJTTJRd0N5bjJHa0FKQ294Wk4yTmJWdXhJdkNod054T2w2Y3dybFhDclA?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[19]</a>. 아울러 LG전자가 엔비디아 파트너 네트워크 냉각 파트너로 합류한 가운데 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMimwFBVV95cUxOM25tcG96aUllNkJaU05sYWhEOExLT3JnQ1ZNTFdqZzFKNnZBMTYtbklVUGtGVkx0S1RZZWhzZ3F1bVpYb3ZaNEZva2EwNWF6NWVqZmtNQWNCT1ZZZ0Q1SEROVXJqUjVXSVpsSE4wdEVTZlFVZjNkbnpHd0RQNFlLWVc4WVJnYzVYT2U1VHlCVU1OZ3VYM0RQdEVxSQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[21]</a> 냉동기 160대 공급을 추진하며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMicEFVX3lxTFB6Zk5HY21jOWVJOXVxZWVpUEFGOXlIUW50Q2h1M3hZSnpZVmU2VkJpeFBzbmduZFdDYXkwMl9XdlJDYWxPaUEtTFNKUzdlM1BxaWRQLUJWLU1tU2FsWUlaZGpiWkYwUXdrMUFvdlJBYW4?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[22]</a> 밸류체인 진입을 가속화함 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=OTEC Carrier Selected as Preferred Negotiator to Supply Cooling Equipment for 200-MW Gumi AI Data Center&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[23]</a>.<div style='margin-top:18px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>3. 구글 인디애나 390MW 승인과 전력망 비용 분담 갈등 및 모라토리엄 침체 경고</div>전력망 포화 속에서도 미국 인디애나주 공공서비스규제위원회(IURC)가 구글 데이터센터의 390MW 신규 수요 대응을 위한 130MW 태양광과 460MW·1.84GWh ESS 구축안인 ‘몬로비아 프로젝트’를 최종 승인함 <a href='https://www.aitimes.com/news/articleView.html?idxno=216227' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[24]</a>. 구글이 와이오밍 702MW 연료전지에 20년 장기 전력구매계약(PPA)을 맺고 <a href='https://www.datacenterdynamics.com/en/news/google-signs-20-year-ppa-with-aep-for-702mw-wyoming-fuel-cell-project/' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[25]</a> 에너플렉스가 1GW급 발전 계약을 수주하는 등 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMirwFBVV95cUxNaDRjTUlzcFVtNGh0NnFadVZOZVItQjBlX2ZFQkM2Z2UwTk9QVmtZcUNURUpDUHhKdjhtVnRVRmFaSDUwcEVBTGVpVjNUSFc1MkxHZ1RJZ2NRc091N3dPVUFTazRTM0RhaTBaaW9CMTF0RDA1enlBVHR1TGZqd2hFMFJWcVk1aG1nYUpYYTVIU2pXd3ZINlFad08zelM4R0o2cWpGY01tdE8yaWhhdldZ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; Kotek orders halt to data center air, water permitting: ‘It is effectively a temporary moratorium’&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[28]</a> 지자체 저항이 극에 달함. 이에 래리 핑크 블랙록 회장은 데이터센터 건설 모라토리엄이 장기화될 경우 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiqAFBVV95cUxQRUcxYlJ4clFzek9WSjhrbnA5Y1pKNjFNOVJXdlVjek5GRGJHMFRQVnJRSWxPamhqNHpFM3VCek9rOHVnbjhQcGhXOVgzdWRTWjZXcDE1OVk0Z3I4bjNXeXRTVDlsMkxYZXZnbVlBeW1HclpRV2lPMFhnb3JVYnV0aXJYaTN0NHY3TmNidWVYLVFFd0JXX0thajhIeVJmZVI0VTdUXzVselI?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; KT 800Gbps AI 전용회선 상용화와 초광대역 패브릭 및 빅테크 설비투자 명암</div>분산된 하이퍼스케일 클러스터를 단일 슈퍼컴퓨터로 통합하기 위한 초광대역 데이터센터 상호연결(DCI) 혁신이 가속화됨 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMikgFBVV95cUxOZEY1bV9nNU9LazY5UEFKZVhxeHpSeWtocVlDSEhRU1ZIM016dDR3Zy0tcWdvOWQ4V05qS0FaRkFPUHdwd3k1SVl3QzEzUnd2VkZEUTZCcVBHRm1IV1hCZG1nM1Jhc3phZFBmMVNUdm9PZXlLR1RyaVkzRUthTU5xa0tUZmtWdDh2bDlJeEhjc0cxZw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7;
